# Implementation issue ledger

This ledger is the chronological index of implementation and verification findings for this change. It complements the detailed evidence in `background.md`, `verification.md`, and `fork-limitations.md`: no entry below is a waived test failure. Each entry identifies whether it is resolved, under investigation, a current-fork contract, or future selective-2.38 work.

PowerShell rejected the unknown parameter before validating any record. | **Resolved invocation-contract issue**: inspect the script interface, rerun with `-ProjectRoot` and `-RequireClean`, and reject the parameter error as validation evidence. | Corrected execution exits zero and reports 0 violations in `audits/planning-record-violations.csv`. |
| SDK-QUALITY-214 | `Frontend/AngelscriptNativeTokenizerCoreTests.cpp`; exact one-line-comment input | Adding the direct public `asIScriptEngine::ParseToken` contract shifted the existing nine-byte `"// hello\n"` input from line 75 to line 102. The inline-source exception registry is intentionally line-keyed, so the stale location produced one `EscapedNewlineConcatenation` violation even though the source bytes and oracle were unchanged. | **Resolved exact-input registration drift**: update only the `ExactTokenizerInput` location and retain the exact trailing LF, explicit byte length 9, and tokenizer assertion. Do not reformat away the behavior under test. | Corrected inline audit reports 296 raw sources, two registered escaped inputs, 296 conforming, and 0 violations. Boundary and source reconciliation remain clean. |

| RESTORE-015 | `FinConstruct`, `DestructScript`, and `CopyScript` script-object type operands were emitted as live process addresses by bytecode-stream version one. A same-source PropertyRebuild pair therefore differed in the serialized operand range even though the source contract was unchanged. | The v1 red trace at `Saved/Tests/as-native-sdk-restore-v1-trace-property-red/20260724_101619_101_c1defee0/Report/index.json` identifies `FinConstruct`, dword index 13, in the exact byte-105–112 operand range, with the first stream difference at byte 111. The writer and reader now canonicalize six type-bearing opcodes (`REFCPY`, `RefCpyV`, `OBJTYPE`, `FinConstruct`, `DestructScript`, `CopyScript`) and publish bytecode-stream version two. The legacy-version test mutates the stream header to v1 and proves rejection with no functions or globals. The v2 PropertyRebuild owner passes 1/1 at `Saved/Tests/as-native-sdk-restore-v2-property-final/20260724_102038_157_f363b24c/Report/index.json`. This is a resolved persistence defect with an intentional breaking stream-format boundary, not backward-compatible loading. |
| RESTORE-016 | The original same-source save/load oracle compared streams made under different module/section identities, treating staging identity as if it were bytecode nondeterminism. | The stream writer stores section identity, so the staging source necessarily differs even with equivalent text. The repaired property test retains strict equality for rebuilds with identical identity and adds two concurrently alive fresh engines using the same module name, section name, source, and configuration as the exact deterministic control. It retains inequality for changed source. The assertion was corrected, not weakened: the fresh control is the valid exact-byte proof, while the staging workflow remains semantic load/execution coverage. |
| RESTORE-017 | A nested non-POD script struct produces real `asBC_CopyScript` bytecode and version-two saves are byte-identical across concurrent identical engines, but this fork rejects the resulting replacement-module load before function bytecode translation. | `CopyScriptSaveDeterminismAndCurrentForkLoadRestriction` passes in `Saved/Tests/as-native-sdk-restore-v2-primitives-contract-final/20260724_103046_900_3b571d8b/Report/index.json`. It prints the full source, observes `asBC_CopyScript`, compares the two streams exactly, then records `Shared type '$obj' doesn't match the original declaration in other module` and `LoadByteCode failed... Number of bytes read ... 86`. This is a current fork loader restriction, not an operand-remapping failure and not a passing nested-object restore. The test asserts rejected load plus zero functions/globals so a later implementation change must deliberately replace this contract with a positive execution test. |
| RESTORE-018 | `DestructScript` is now included in v2 pointer canonicalization because it has the same type-operand representation, but current generated sources do not emit it in a positive persisted path. | Writer/reader symmetry is present and the opcode is covered by the source-level operand contract. A direct persisted execution regression remains pending until an enabled current-fork source can emit the instruction without relying on private engine APIs or unavailable selected-2.38 syntax. This is a coverage gap explicitly retained, not evidence of working emitted behavior. |

## Recording rules

- Retain the command, artifact directory, exact stable product ID, and observed assertion or crash location for every newly found issue before a corrective implementation is accepted.
- A generated source is logged in full through the shared source reporter; concise begin/end markers remain in the automation result. A failure must never be recorded without pointing to its persisted source evidence.
- A current-fork difference is a contract only after direct public API/runtime evidence. A missing expectation, a generator bug, an unexamined diagnostic, and a skipped execution mode are not contracts.
- A repair remains incomplete until its focused verification and the affected parent topic both have evidence. Generated StaticJIT source compilation is not evidence of generated StaticJIT execution.

| LANG-VAR-001 | Variable initializers queried type IDs through `asIScriptEngine`, even when the generated enum and script value type belonged only to the generated module. It also compared raw debug declaration spelling for aliases/floats instead of public metadata. | The 2026-07-24 full report identified eight initializer assertions: engine lookup failed for module-local enum/script types and canonical debug spelling differed for `double`/typedef rows. The repair resolves each type through `asIScriptModule::GetTypeIdByDecl`, compares `GetVar()` type IDs, and retains name-bearing debug-declaration evidence. | Resolved test-oracle defect. Module-local type identity must be queried from its module. | Do not move generated types into engine-global registration or weaken metadata checks to string-only probes. |
| LANG-VAR-002 | Primitive local declarations without an initializer have no stable readable value contract: `CallDefaultConstructor()` returns without emitting initialization for non-object, non-handle values. The first full run accidentally treated a zero read as semantic output. | `as_compiler.cpp` `CallDefaultConstructor()` exits early for non-object/handle values. Printed sources such as `LANG-VAR-INIT-STORAGE-DEFAULT-LOCAL-INT16` declared then compared the uninitialized primitive, producing unstable results. The repaired source keeps the raw declaration, explicitly assigns the zero value before its runtime read, and still exercises declaration/storage/metadata. The FailureBoundary uninitialized-read product remains executed and printed, but only asserts completion/cleanup and logs the observed return. | Enabled base/fork API characterization, not a Disabled case: uninitialized primitive execution has no stable result oracle. | Do not delete uninitialized-declaration products or assert a platform-accidental zero value. |

The repaired product retains every generated source, requires compilation and module metadata for all primitive cells, and deliberately avoids reading their values. | Enabled current-fork parser/compiler characterization; revisit if default const-local semantics are standardized or changed by a 2.38 backport. | Do not silently remove the accepted forms or invent a primitive-value result. |

Owner = F...();` construction while retaining the same class/property/scope/cleanup products. | Resolved fixture construction defect; this is the same explicit-construction requirement already recorded for raw debugger object fixtures. | Do not replace class/property/scope products with native value stand-ins. |
